How to read this log
- Item numbers are the brief's ("要先實作", items 0 to 18).
- <work> is the video's work folder outside the repository, <seed> is <work>/_tools/seed, <lab> is
  <work>/run/lab (the throwaway project, rebuilt from the seed before every session), <plugin> is
  <work>/run/ship-kit (built only for p1, absent in every other session), <logs> is
  <work>/run/logs, <runner> is <work>/_tools/runner, <repo> is the repository checkout. The home
  directory is written <home>. session.sh's own "show" filter writes <lab>, <plugin>, <logs>,
  <seed>, <work>, <home>, <user>, <host>, <address>, <uuid>, toolu_<id>, agent-<id>.
  Two changes were made when this file was put together (the runner's assemble.mjs): the line of
  each record and of each dry run that lists the names of the inherited variables is replaced by
  "## variables this shell hands the session: N names (the list is left out of this log) | of
  the script's five removed for the session: N", and trailing blanks were removed. Nothing else
  in a quoted output was changed.
- WORK was set to the video's work folder for every command, written the POSIX way (/c/...).
  <lab> lies below the home directory: 8 folders above it (session.sh's count; one has a
  .claude/skills folder and one has .claude settings, which the script counts and never opens;
  none has a CLAUDE.md, an AGENTS.md, a .mcp.json, a .claude-plugin or a .git).
- "[exit N]" is the exit code captured right after a command.
- "runner" marks a check that is not in the brief. The runner's scripts live in <runner>:
    one.sh <name> <arm>   runs "WORK=<work> bash <seed>/session.sh <name> <arm>" (never --dry,
                          never --force; refuses a name it has started before), keeps what it
                          printed, then runs scan.mjs. Sessions were started one at a time and the
                          output was read before the next one was started.
    scan.mjs              where each model name appears (init, main-conversation messages,
                          messages under an Agent call, modelUsage keys); the files the run left
                          in <logs>; the inherited-variable line as a count and a digest; the
                          project's files against the listing taken before the session.
    lists.mjs             the init lists of every session split three ways (Claude Code's own /
                          the seed's / any other, a count), and which shipped skills are listed in
                          some sessions and not in others.
    results.mjs, gather.sh, card.sh, diff-mchecks.sh, build-demo.sh, demo-check.sh, assemble.mjs
                          the tables, the small result files in demo/results/, item 17, the
                          comparison of the checks with the reviser's record, demo/ and this file.
  Copies of one.sh, scan.mjs, lists.mjs, results.mjs, gather.sh, card.sh and diff-mchecks.sh are in
  demo/runner/ next to the seed's four; in the copies the one line that held the work folder's
  path reads W="${WORK:?...}". They are kept as a record of what was run, not as a tool for
  viewers: they expect the work folder's layout, not demo/'s.
- Raw files (stream.jsonl, debug.txt, stderr.txt, seen.txt, session.txt, <name>.lab/) stay in
  <logs>, outside the repository.
- The streams carry rate-limit events. No script here reads or prints them. No session id,
  message uuid or tool-call id is quoted (no session made a tool call). The debug records were
  read only through the seed's loader-seen.mjs (counts).
- "Any other" (a plugin, skill, agent, MCP server or MCP tool that is neither the seed's nor Claude
  Code's own) is 0 in all ten sessions; the table is in ITEM 12. Instruction files loaded: only
  the project's own CLAUDE.md, in t1 and m1; none in the other eight; none from outside the
  project in any.
- Names of Claude Code's own entries are not recorded, with one exception that changes the
  figures: z1 lists one shipped skill that no later session lists. It was looked up on the seed's
  list of shipped skills: it is "slides" (ITEM 12).

Before any session (step 1 of the runner's task): what was read, and what it can touch
- Read in full: the brief (1,160 lines), session.sh, kit.sh, m-checks.sh, ctx-cost.mjs,
  measure-seed.mjs, standin-names.sh, runner/after.mjs, runner/said.mjs, runner/loader-seen.mjs,
  watch/seen.mjs, watch/watch-hooks.json, parts/guard.mjs, parts/note.mjs,
  parts/hooks.project.json, parts/hooks.plugin.json, parts/hooks.note.json,
  parts/mcp.project.json, parts/manifest.json, parts/log-scout.agent.md,
  parts/release-prep.skill.md, parts/release-prep.template.md, parts/memory.team.md, the four
  request files, every file under lab/ and placed/, the reviewers' findings and the reviser's
  scan-names.mjs. Not read line by line: the five check-*.mjs self-tests and
  runner/context-read.mjs (no session can start them; the self-tests were run, ITEM 2; the
  context arm was not run).
- Four programs can be started by a session, and no other. All are node scripts of the seed, each
  started by a command the seed fixes ("node" plus one argument); none takes text from the model
  as a command, none opens a network connection, none starts another program, none installs
  anything; each imports node's built-in modules only (node:fs, node:os, node:path,
  node:readline, node:url).
    watch/seen.mjs (in the project: .claude/hooks/seen.mjs; registered for eight hook events by
      .claude/settings.local.json in every arm): reads the hook event from stdin; appends one line
      to the file SEEN_LOG names (session.sh sets it to <logs>/<name>.seen.txt; without the
      variable, .claude/seen.txt in the project); prints nothing on stdout. It writes names only
      when they are the seed's (release-prep, log-scout, gear's tools) or a built-in agent's, and
      a path only when it is inside the project or the plugin. One case stops a call: PreToolUse
      of Read, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit, Glob or Grep whose file_path / notebook_path / path, or
      whose Glob pattern / Grep glob when it starts with a drive letter, a slash, ~ or .., lies
      outside the project and the plugin folder: the line ends " | BLOCKED", one line without a
      path goes to stderr, exit code 2.
    parts/guard.mjs (h1 and t1: .claude/hooks/guard.mjs, PreToolUse on Edit|Write; p1: the plugin's
      scripts/guard.mjs): reads the event from stdin, asks whether the target is an existing
      *.test.js / .mjs / .cjs file (existsSync, nothing is opened), appends one line with the
      tool name and the target's base name to the file GUARD_LOG names (<logs>/<name>.guard.txt),
      and exits 2 with two lines on stderr only for such a file.
    parts/note.mjs (n1 only, not run): appends one line to the file NOTE_LOG names and prints
      three fixed lines.
    lab/server/gear.mjs (t1, c1, u1: started by Claude Code as the stdio MCP server the project's
      .mcp.json names, "node server/gear.mjs"): reads server/stock.tsv next to itself, answers
      initialize, ping, tools/list and tools/call from that table, appends one line per message
      to server/requests.txt in the project.
  So the only places a hook or the server writes are <logs> (three record files) and the project
  (server/requests.txt). Confirmed after every session: the runner's scan.mjs lists what the run
  left in <logs> and what changed in the project (only server/requests.txt, in t1, c1, u1).
- Bash and PowerShell are not offered to the model in any arm: every command has
  --tools Read,Glob,Grep,Edit,Write,Skill,Agent,Task,ToolSearch, and the init events confirm it.
- Every arm passes --permission-mode default (one line of session.sh builds it for all fourteen
  arms). No arm passes bypassPermissions, acceptEdits, auto or --dangerously-skip-permissions.
  The prepared "safe" arm adds --safe-mode, which loads less, not more; it was not run.
- The five file tools are pre-approved without a path, so the permission mode does not keep them
  inside the project; the logging hook's block does, and after.mjs's verdict part seven says STOP
  when the stream or the hook's record holds a file-tool call aimed outside (a blocked one
  counts). Read in the code and exercised by check-seen.mjs (5 blocked of 30 made-up events) and
  check-after.mjs in ITEM 2. No real session made any tool call, so the block was never needed.
- The requests ask for nothing outside the project: idle.txt asks for an answer without tools;
  work.txt (not run) names the release, logs/ and a stock code.
- session.sh itself: builds <lab> and <plugin> by deleting and copying, refuses a run folder
  inside a git repository (exit 4, by looking for a .git entry; it starts no git command) and a
  folder it did not build (exit 5), never reuses a name (exit 3), writes its record through the
  replacements listed above, and compares three listings before and after the session.
- The reading held: nothing above is false, so the sessions were started. Three small things
  the reading turned up, none of which came into play (no session made a tool call):
    (1) the guard and the logging hook are both PreToolUse hooks; if a model aimed Edit or Write
        outside the project, the guard could ask the file system whether that path exists (and
        write its base name to <logs>) while the logging hook blocks the call;
    (2) KIT_DIR is set in every arm, so the logging hook and after.mjs would treat a path under
        <work>/run/ship-kit as "inside" even in the arms where that folder does not exist;
    (3) m-checks.sh's stand-in standin-names.sh writes this machine's names to a stderr file in a
        temporary folder to test the replacements, and removes the folder at the end.

- z1, the first session of the day in this folder, lists one shipped skill that none of the nine
  later sessions lists: "slides". Every other count is what the arm should hold. Tools, agents and
  plugins of Claude Code's own are the same in all ten (8 tools, 7 in u1; 6 agents; 3 plugins).

  h1 (a hook that was never triggered) is 11,186, 4 below z2; z1 is 80 above z2. The plugins
  video saw the same extra skill in its first session and recorded that the total did not move;
  here it moved by about 80.
- Why the first session lists it and the later ones do not was not looked into (it would mean
  reading Claude Code's own files or records). It is not the seed's and not the owner's: it is on
  the seed's list of skills Claude Code ships.

Read by the rules as fixed:
- base = mean of z1 and z2 = 11,230; spread 80; bar = max(80, 11) = 80; threshold for the sums =
  2 x 80 + 10 = 170.
- standing cost of each part (arm minus base):
    CLAUDE.md (m1)                                   +263
    Skill (s1)                                       +21    cannot tell (within 80 tokens)
    subagent (a1)                                    +12    cannot tell (within 80 tokens)
    first deferred MCP server (c1)                   +671
    the same server, two tools loaded at once (u1)   +208
    hook, registered and not triggered (h1)          -44    cannot tell (within 80 tokens)
    plugin (p1)                                      +85
    all five (t1)                                    +1,089
- the five against all together: (m1 + s1 + a1 + c1 + h1) - 5 x base = 263 + 21 + 12 + 671 - 44 =
  923; t1 - base = 1,089; all together is 166 more than the sum; threshold 170: inside it.
- the plugin against its parts: (s1 + a1 + h1) - 3 x base = 21 + 12 - 44 = -11; p1 - base = +85;
  the plugin is 96 more than the sum; threshold 170: inside it.
- c1 with u1: c1 - base = +671, u1 - base = +208, c1 - u1 = 463, more than the bar. The brief's
  second case: "c1 比 u1 大、而且大超過尺：多出來的不是這個伺服器的兩個工具". (u1 is above the base,
  so the fourth case does not apply.) Debug records, tool search | deferred: z1 2 | 1, c1 1 | 1,
  u1 1 | 0.
- the largest row is c1 (+671); the other rows of the first table together are 263 + 21 + 12 -
  44 = 252, and with the plugin 337: c1 is larger than the other five together.
These figures are what the fixed rules give, and they are weak: with a bar of 80 the Skill and
the subagent rows cannot be told from nothing, and a threshold of 170 would pass almost any sum.
The cause is arithmetic, not noise: z1 carries about 80 tokens the other nine runs do not, so the
mean base is about 40 too high for every arm; the sum of five arms takes that 40 five times and
t1 takes it once (5 x 40 - 40 = 160 of the 166).

Which usage field carried the differences:
- input_tokens is 2 in every one of the ten first requests. It carries nothing.

  changes from run to run: z1, t1 and u1 are all cache write with 0 read (z1 is the first session
  in the folder; t1 and u1 hold a set of tools no earlier session had sent); the other seven read
  between 5,649 and 7,071 and write between 4,382 and 5,842.

  write (4,386, 4,382, 4,438, 4,511). s1 reads 6,536, m1 5,649 and c1 7,071: there the read side
  moved too. Neither field alone gives a part's cost; only the sum of the three does.

  (same lists, a hook that never ran): 11,190 and 11,186, 4 apart.

AT THE STOP  停下來那時寫的「與企劃不同或企劃時不知道的事」（十次之後；最後的版本在檔尾）
================================================================================================
1. 兩次基準差 80，不是 10 以內。z1（這個資料夾今天第一次 session）的 init 多列一個 Claude Code
   自己的 skill「slides」，之後九次都沒有（skills 22 對 21、slash commands 58 對 57）。企劃引外掛
   那支的紀錄說多一個內建 skill「總 token 沒有跟著變」；這一次跟著變了，約 80。照第 12 項的
   規定停在 z2，w1、n1 沒有跑。
2. 照跑之前講定的規則，尺變成 80、相加的門檻變成 170：Skill（+21）、subagent（+12）、hook（-44）
   三列都是「分不出來（80 個 token 以內）」，兩個相加都「在門檻以內」（差 166 與 96），這個
   「以內」沒有說明力。原因是算術：基準的平均被 z1 墊高約 40，每一臂都少算 40。
3. 以 z2 一次當基準（不是企劃的規則，只供決定下一步）：CLAUDE.md +303、Skill +61、subagent +52、
   第一個延後載入的 MCP 伺服器 +711、同一個伺服器一次載入 +248、hook -4、外掛 +125、全部 +1,129；
   五樣相加 1,123 對 1,129（差 6），外掛的三樣 109 對 125（差 16）。h1 與 z2 只差 4。
4. c1 比預期小：企劃預期多 900 到 1,200，量到 +671（對 z2 是 +711）。u1 在企劃猜的 200 到 450
   之內（+208／+248）。c1 減 u1 是 463：讀數規則的第二種情形（多出來的不是這個伺服器的兩個
   工具）。c1 仍是表上最大的一列，比其他五樣加起來多（671 對 337；對 z2 是 711 對 537）。
5. input_tokens 在十次的第一個請求都是 2。差值全在 cache 寫入與讀取兩欄，而且兩欄怎麼分每次
   不同：z1、t1、u1 是全部寫入、讀取 0；z2、h1、a1、p1 四次讀取都是 6,802，差值只在寫入那一欄；
   m1、s1、c1 連讀取那一欄也不同。t1 是同一個資料夾的第二次 session，讀取仍是 0。
6. t1、s1 的 skills 總數沒有比 z1 多 1（都是 22）：種子的一個加上內建的 21 個。企劃第 4 項、
   第 5–11 項寫的「比 z1 多 1」不成立，原因同第 1 點。
7. u1 的 init 沒有 ToolSearch（內建工具 7 個），verdict 第三段是「ToolSearch listed: no」；
   偵錯紀錄 deferred 的行數 z1 是 1、c1 是 1、u1 是 0，tool search 的行數 z1 是 2、c1 與 u1 是 1。
8. 第 17 項第二行（head -8）印出來的第七、八行是「-- after the last request:」與
   「other system lines: 1」，不是企劃寫的兩行 result：每一次的串流在唯一的請求之後都有一行

仍然沒有觀察到
================================================================================================
- 為什麼當天第一次 session 多列一個內建 skill、之後不列；它什麼時候會再出現；觀眾自己量的時候
  會不會碰到（沒有的兩次裡第一次比較大）。只知道這一次與外掛那支的第一次都是這樣。
- 每一列「每次都是」這個數：基準（清單相同的）兩次，其餘每一臂一次；w1 一次。
- 每一種零件單獨被用到時主對話多多少：w1 是四種混在一次裡，而且前三種的呼叫在同一則訊息。
- ToolSearch 載入兩個工具定義時進請求的量：它的工具結果只有 1 個字元的文字，第二個請求多的
  1,568 是好幾樣合在一起的，拆不出來。
- 守門 hook 擋下一次呼叫時進來的東西（四次都放行）；PreToolUse、PostToolUse 的 stdout 進不進
  脈絡（沒有任何一次 hook 有輸出）。
- 記錄 hook 擋下專案外的檔案工具呼叫在真的 session 裡長什麼樣：十三次裡沒有任何一筆指到
  專案外。
- n1 印的字在之後的請求裡還在不在：n1 只有一個請求。
- subagent 在前景跑的形狀（這一次是背景）；自己讀對交給 subagent 的對照。
- 「改完再量一次」的前後對照（成果 4 的後半）：這支沒有跑成對的兩次，引 Skills 那支。
- x1（-p 之下的 /context）、slash、safe 三臂；claude plugin details；互動式的畫面（/context
  的格子、/usage、狀態列）；第 18 項的第一次使用者檢查。
- 企劃「沒有觀察到的事」那一節列的其餘各項（很長的 CLAUDE.md、好幾個 Skill、平常 session 裡的
  第一個 MCP 伺服器、壓縮、其他模型與平台、金額換算、時間），照舊。


## Coordinator note, 2026-10-11T05:00Z
On point 17 above and point 6 of the brief's run record, which say the ToolSearch tool result in w1
is "1 character" and that what it loaded cannot be seen: the first fact check read the raw stream.
The result is two tool_reference blocks naming the seed server's two tools and holds no text at all.
The "1" is the newline ctx-cost.mjs puts between two empty strings when it joins a result's text
parts. So the result does show which tools were loaded; what the stream does not show is the size
of their definitions. The script in demo/ is left as it ran; the video says "two tool references,
no text". Nothing above this note was changed and no session was run for it.
